1	# Auxilio Workspace
2	
3	Google **Workspace Add-on** for Auxilio (Office Ops). Lets a host register an in-person visit **from inside the Google Calendar event** — the Google-sanctioned, mobile-capable version of the "Visit tab" idea. This is a **channel surface**, not a backend: it renders cards and forwards intent to `office-ops-engine`.
4	
5	> Read the umbrella `../CLAUDE.md` for the four constraints and UX rules. They apply here too.
6	
7	## What this is (and isn't)
8	
9	- **Is:** an Apps Script add-on (CardService) in the Calendar right rail on **web + the mobile Calendar app**. **The whole host flow lives in the card** (card-first, D5) — roster, per-guest include toggle, name/phone edits, template + location pickers, email preview, send, manage — via card form inputs + action handlers that call the engine over HTTPS.
10	- **Isn't:** a place for business logic. Dedup, extraction, invite/pass issuance, and lifecycle flows all live in `office-ops-engine`'s visitor plugin. This repo is a **dumb adapter** (per the umbrella architecture).
11	- **Isn't (v1):** a web app. We **do not** bounce the host to a browser tab — that breaks "everything in front of the user," worst on mobile. A full web editor is **deferred/optional** (D6), built only on evidence the card ceiling is real.
12	
13	## Hard rules (decided 2026-06-16)
14	
15	1. **Contract lives in the engine, not here.** The add-on POSTs to `POST /api/visitor/calendar/addon/register` with a Google ID token; the engine verifies it, normalizes to a `NormalizedCalendarEvent{ source: 'marketplace' }`, and feeds the existing spine `ingestCalendarEvent` — the same one the `.ics`/magic-address ingest calls. Dedup is on `tenantId + iCalUID` (the `calendar_event_links` ledger), so the add-on path and the room-resource path converge instead of duplicating. **Send the canonical `iCalUID`** (from the Advanced Calendar Service `Calendar.Events.get(...).iCalUID`) so convergence actually works. Full contract: `../office-ops-engine/spec/2026-06-16_calendar_addon_register_endpoint.md`.
16	2. **Internal-first.** Published privately to the Appointy Workspace domain (admin one-click push to all users — no per-user install). **No Google OAuth verification needed while internal.** Public Marketplace listing is deferred packaging over this same code.
17	3. **Two-way without broad OAuth.** Use the narrow current-event scopes:
18	   - `addOns.calendar.currentEventAccess = READ_WRITE`
19	   - `.../auth/calendar.addons.current.event.read`, `.../auth/calendar.addons.current.event.write`
20	
21	   Attendees arrive in the trigger payload (no Calendar API call). `READ_WRITE` lets us write the pass link / "✓ Registered" back onto the *open* event. Broad calendar OAuth stays in the engine's ingest pipeline — **do not duplicate it here.**
22	4. **Calendar-only scopes. Never add Gmail or Drive.** Calendar is a *sensitive* scope (verification but no paid audit). Gmail/Drive are *restricted* → would trigger the annual CASA security assessment. This is a hard line.
23	
24	## Triggers (manifest)
25	
26	- `calendar.eventOpenTrigger` → first render of the visitor card (attendees from payload, no backend call).
27	- `calendar.eventUpdateTrigger` → re-render on attendee add/remove; surface the per-guest include toggle (the online/offline solution).
28	- action handlers (edit/send) → `UrlFetchApp` POST to `POST /api/visitor/calendar/addon/*` → re-rendered card; then write confirmation back to the event.
29	- `calendar.homepageTrigger` (Phase 3, optional) → standalone "today's visitors" panel.
30	
31	## Stack & deploy
32	
33	- **Runtime:** Google Apps Script (CardService). Google-hosted — **₹0 infra.**
34	- **Deploy:** `clasp` (Google Apps Script CLI) → Google. **Not Bifrost, not EAS.** The umbrella's "push to `personal` remote for Bifrost deploys" rule does **not** apply here.
35	- **Auth to engine:** the action handler sends the host's identity (ID token); the engine verifies it so a host can only register visits for their own tenant.
36	
37	## Status
38	
39	**Phase 1 MVP card exists** (card-first): `appsscript.json` (manifest) + `Code.js` (CardService card — roster with include toggles + phone, preview, save & send; calls the engine with `getIdentityToken()`). Engine Phase 0 is implemented + tested. To install/test locally see **`SETUP.md`** (ngrok tunnel → local engine, real OAuth token).
40	
41	Canonical plan: `../Planning-docs/2026-06-16_calendar_visitor_addon_build_plan.md`. Engine contract: `../office-ops-engine/spec/2026-06-16_calendar_addon_register_endpoint.md`. First version of `Code.js` was written outside Google — refine in the Apps Script editor (it can run/log there).
42	
```

1	/**
2	 * Auxilio Visitor — Google Calendar add-on (card-first).
3	 *
4	 * The whole host flow lives in this card; it calls office-ops-engine over HTTPS.
5	 * Engine contract: ../office-ops-engine/spec/2026-06-16_calendar_addon_register_endpoint.md
6	 *
7	 * Apps Script (GAS, V8) — flat global scope, no imports/modules.
8	 * First version: push with clasp, then refine in the Apps Script editor (it can run
9	 * and log there; it can't be run from the monorepo).
10	 */
11	
12	// Engine base URL. Default = the local-tunnel domain (npm run backendtunnel).
13	// Must also be listed in appsscript.json → urlFetchWhitelist.
14	var ENGINE_BASE = 'https://unyearning-olympia-unclimactic.ngrok-free.dev';
15	
16	// Appointy logo embedded as a base64 data URI (gradient original from appointy.svg).
17	// Source: appointy.svg (copied from visitor-web/public/, also in this repo).
18	// Used by setImageUrl() in all card headers.
19	var ADDON_ICON_URL = 'data:image/svg+xml;base64,REDACTED';
20	
21	// Primary brand colour — used for filled buttons and accent text.
22	var BRAND_COLOR = '#92288E';
23	
24	// ── Triggers ──────────────────────────────────────────────
25	function onCalendarEventOpen(e) { return [buildVisitorCard_(e)]; }
26	function onCalendarEventUpdate(e) { return [buildVisitorCard_(e)]; }
27	
28	// Serves the branded icon SVG at ?asset=logo. ContentService.MimeType.XML is the
29	// closest available type to image/svg+xml in GAS. The manifest logoUrl below points
30	// to visitor-web instead, which nginx serves with the correct image/svg+xml header.
31	function doGet(e) {
32	  if (e && e.parameter && e.parameter.asset === 'logo') {
33	    var b64 = ADDON_ICON_URL.split(',')[1];
34	    var svgText = Utilities.newBlob(Utilities.base64Decode(b64)).getDataAsString();
35	    return ContentService.createTextOutput(svgText).setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.XML);
36	  }
37	  return ContentService.createTextOutput('Auxilio Workspace Add-on');
38	}
39	
40	// Three-dot menu → Sign out.
41	// NOTE: we deliberately do NOT call ScriptApp.invalidateAuth() — invalidating auth
42	// breaks every subsequent add-on invocation (incl. re-clicking this very item) with
43	// "Unspecified error invoking the add-on", because the platform can't run any
44	// function once authorization is cleared. A Calendar add-on also has no login page
45	// we can navigate to (Google only shows the consent prompt automatically on the next
46	// authorized open). So sign-out routes the user to the real, linkable destination:
47	// the Google Account permissions page, where they can remove access / switch account.
48	// Removing access there forces full re-consent the next time they open the add-on.
49	function onSignOut(e) {
50	  var card = CardService.newCardBuilder()
51	    .setHeader(CardService.newCardHeader()
52	      .setTitle('Sign out')
53	      .setImageUrl(ADDON_ICON_URL))
54	    .addSection(CardService.newCardSection()
55	      .addWidget(CardService.newDecoratedText()
56	        .setStartIcon(CardService.newIconImage().setIcon(CardService.Icon.PERSON))
57	        .setText('Switch account or re-grant access')
58	        .setBottomLabel('Remove Auxilio Visitor from your Google account, then reopen the add-on — you\'ll be asked to sign in and grant access again.')
59	        .setWrapText(true))
60	      .addWidget(CardService.newTextButton()
61	        .setText('Manage access')
62	        .setTextButtonStyle(CardService.TextButtonStyle.FILLED)
63	        .setOpenLink(CardService.newOpenLink()
64	          .setUrl('https://myaccount.google.com/permissions'))))
65	    .build();
66	  return CardService.newUniversalActionResponseBuilder()
67	    .displayAddOnCards([card])
68	    .build();
69	}
70	
71	function onHomepage(e) {
72	  var introSection = CardService.newCardSection()
73	    .addWidget(CardService.newTextParagraph()
74	      .setText('Open a calendar event with external guests to register visitor passes.'));
75	
76	  var howSection = CardService.newCardSection()
77	    .setHeader('How it works')
78	    .addWidget(CardService.newDecoratedText()
79	      .setStartIcon(CardService.newIconImage().setIcon(CardService.Icon.PERSON))
80	      .setText('Choose who gets a pass')
81	      .setBottomLabel('Toggle guests on or off from the event\'s attendee list.')
82	      .setWrapText(true))
83	    .addWidget(CardService.newDecoratedText()
84	      .setStartIcon(CardService.newIconImage().setIcon(CardService.Icon.INVITE))
85	      .setText('Review and send')
86	      .setBottomLabel('Edit names and phone numbers, preview the invite email, then send.')
87	      .setWrapText(true))
88	    .addWidget(CardService.newDecoratedText()
89	      .setStartIcon(CardService.newIconImage().setIcon(CardService.Icon.TICKET))
90	      .setText('Manage anytime')
91	      .setBottomLabel('Cancel or resend passes by toggling them off and on.')
92	      .setWrapText(true));
93	
94	  return CardService.newCardBuilder()
95	    .setHeader(CardService.newCardHeader()
96	      .setTitle('Auxilio Visitor')
97	      .setSubtitle('Visitor passes from your calendar')
98	      .setImageUrl(ADDON_ICON_URL))
99	    .addSection(introSection)
100	    .addSection(howSection)
101	    .build();
102	}
103	
104	// ── Engine HTTP ───────────────────────────────────────────
105	function engineFetch_(path, method, payload) {
106	  var options = {
107	    method: method,
108	    contentType: 'application/json',
109	    muteHttpExceptions: true,
110	    headers: {
111	      Authorization: 'Bearer ' + ScriptApp.getIdentityToken(),
112	      'ngrok-skip-browser-warning': 'true'
113	    }
114	  };
115	  if (payload) options.payload = JSON.stringify(payload);
116	  var res = UrlFetchApp.fetch(ENGINE_BASE + path, options);
117	  var json = {};
118	  try { json = JSON.parse(res.getContentText()); } catch (err) {}
119	  return { code: res.getResponseCode(), json: json };
120	}
121	
122	// Resolve attendees + (best-effort) iCalUID/title/times for the open event.
123	// Attendees come from the add-on payload (currentEventAccess) — no API call needed.
124	// The Calendar API lookup enriches with the canonical iCalUID + title/times so the
125	// engine dedups with the .ics/OAuth paths; if it fails we fall back to the event id
126	// as the key so the flow still works.
127	function loadEvent_(e) {
128	  var cal = e.calendar || {};
129	  var calendarId = cal.calendarId;
130	  var eventId = cal.id;
131	  var payloadAtt = cal.attendees || [];
132	  var info = {
133	    iCalUid: eventId,
134	    providerEventId: eventId,
135	    keySource: 'eventId',
136	    title: '',
137	    start: null,
138	    end: null,
139	    attendees: mapAttendees_(payloadAtt),
140	    debug: ''
141	  };
142	  var ev = fetchEventWithRetry_(calendarId, eventId);
143	  if (ev) {
144	    if (ev.iCalUID) { info.iCalUid = ev.iCalUID; info.keySource = 'iCalUID'; }
145	    info.title = ev.summary || '';
146	    info.start = ev.start ? (ev.start.dateTime || ev.start.date) : null;
147	    info.end = ev.end ? (ev.end.dateTime || ev.end.date) : null;
148	    var apiAtt = mapAttendees_(ev.attendees);
149	    if (info.attendees.length === 0 && apiAtt.length > 0) info.attendees = apiAtt;
150	  }
151	  info.debug = 'key=' + info.keySource + ' guests=' + info.attendees.length;
152	  return info;
153	}
154	
155	// Resolve the event from the Calendar API, tolerating transient 404s: retry once
156	// after a short wait (eventual consistency) and try the primary calendar as a
157	// fallback for the calendarId. Returns the event resource or null.
158	function fetchEventWithRetry_(calendarId, eventId) {
159	  var targets = [];
160	  if (calendarId) targets.push(calendarId);
161	  if (calendarId !== 'primary') targets.push('primary');
162	  for (var attempt = 0; attempt < 2; attempt++) {
163	    for (var t = 0; t < targets.length; t++) {
164	      try {
165	        var ev = Calendar.Events.get(targets[t], eventId);
166	        if (ev) return ev;
167	      } catch (err) {
168	        // 404 / transient → fall through to the next target / retry.
169	      }
170	    }
171	    if (attempt === 0) Utilities.sleep(500);
172	  }
173	  return null;
174	}
175	
176	// Per-guest avatar: Gravatar keyed on the email (real photo if they have one),
177	// falling back to a coloured initials avatar (ui-avatars) when they don't. Rendered
178	// as a circular image icon. Image URLs are fetched by Google's client, so they
179	// don't need urlFetchWhitelist.
180	function avatarIcon_(email, name) {
181	  var initials = (name || email || '?').trim().charAt(0).toUpperCase() || '?';
182	  var fallback = 'https://ui-avatars.com/api/?name=' + encodeURIComponent(initials) +
183	    '&size=64&background=92288E&color=ffffff&bold=true';
184	  var url = 'https://www.gravatar.com/avatar/' + md5Hex_((email || '').trim().toLowerCase()) +
185	    '?s=64&d=' + encodeURIComponent(fallback);
186	  return CardService.newIconImage()
187	    .setIconUrl(url)
188	    .setImageCropType(CardService.ImageCropType.CIRCLE);
189	}
190	
191	// Minimal MD5 → lowercase hex (Gravatar needs MD5 of the email).
192	function md5Hex_(str) {
193	  var bytes = Utilities.computeDigest(Utilities.DigestAlgorithm.MD5, str, Utilities.Charset.UTF_8);
194	  var hex = '';
195	  for (var i = 0; i < bytes.length; i++) {
196	    var b = (bytes[i] + 256) % 256;
197	    hex += (b < 16 ? '0' : '') + b.toString(16);
198	  }
199	  return hex;
200	}
201	
202	function mapAttendees_(list) {
203	  return (list || [])
204	    .filter(function (a) { return a && a.email && !a.self && !a.organizer && !a.resource; })
205	    .map(function (a) {
206	      // Google often sets displayName = the email for non-contacts → treat as no name.
207	      var nm = (a.displayName && a.displayName !== a.email) ? a.displayName : '';
208	      return { email: a.email, name: nm };
209	    });
210	}
211	
212	// ── Card helpers ──────────────────────────────────────────
213	
214	function statusLabel_(g) {
215	  if (g.status === 'sent')      return '<font color="#34a853">Pass sent</font>';
216	  if (g.status === 'cancelled') return '<font color="#ea4335">Cancelled — toggle on to resend</font>';
217	  return g.include === false
218	    ? '<font color="#9aa0a6">No pass</font>'
219	    : '<font color="' + BRAND_COLOR + '">Will get a pass</font>';
220	}
221	
222	// Returns a coloured stat line: "3 guests · 2 sent · 1 pending"
223	function buildStatLine_(roster) {
224	  var total = roster.length;
225	  var sent = 0, pending = 0, cancelled = 0;
226	  for (var i = 0; i < roster.length; i++) {
227	    var g = roster[i];
228	    if (g.status === 'sent') sent++;
229	    else if (g.status === 'cancelled') cancelled++;
230	    else if (g.include !== false) pending++;
231	  }
232	  var parts = [total + (total === 1 ? ' guest' : ' guests')];
233	  if (sent)      parts.push('<font color="#34a853">' + sent + ' sent</font>');
234	  if (pending)   parts.push('<font color="' + BRAND_COLOR + '">' + pending + ' pending</font>');
235	  if (cancelled) parts.push('<font color="#ea4335">' + cancelled + ' cancelled</font>');
236	  return parts.join('  ·  ');
237	}
238	
239	// Returns "Meeting title · Wed 18 Jun, 3:00 PM" for the card subtitle.
240	function formatEventSubtitle_(ev) {
241	  if (!ev.start) return ev.title || '';
242	  try {
243	    var d = new Date(ev.start);
244	    var day  = d.toLocaleDateString('en-IN', { weekday: 'short', day: 'numeric', month: 'short' });
245	    var time = d.toLocaleTimeString('en-IN', { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit', hour12: true });
246	    var datePart = day + ', ' + time;
247	    return ev.title ? ev.title + '  ·  ' + datePart : datePart;
248	  } catch (err) {
249	    return ev.title || '';
250	  }
251	}
252	
253	// ── Main card ─────────────────────────────────────────────
254	function buildVisitorCard_(e) {
255	  // Fast path: no external guests in the payload → render immediately without any
256	  // network call. Keeps empty events snappy so the loader never lingers.
257	  var cal = e.calendar || {};
258	  if (mapAttendees_(cal.attendees).length === 0) {
259	    return CardService.newCardBuilder()
260	      .setHeader(CardService.newCardHeader()
261	        .setTitle('Visitor passes')
262	        .setImageUrl(ADDON_ICON_URL))
263	      .addSection(CardService.newCardSection()
264	        .addWidget(CardService.newDecoratedText()
265	          .setStartIcon(CardService.newIconImage().setIcon(CardService.Icon.PERSON))
266	          .setText('No guests yet')
267	          .setBottomLabel('Add a guest to this event.')
268	          .setWrapText(true)))
269	      .build();
270	  }
271	
272	  var ev = loadEvent_(e);
273	  if (!ev.iCalUid) return errorCard_('No event id in payload (' + ev.debug + ') — save the event, then reopen.');
274	
275	  var organizerEmail = (cal.organizer && cal.organizer.email) || '';
276	  var res = engineFetch_('/api/visitor/calendar/addon/draft', 'POST', {
277	    iCalUid: ev.iCalUid, providerEventId: ev.providerEventId, organizerEmail: organizerEmail,
278	    title: ev.title, start: ev.start, end: ev.end, attendees: ev.attendees
279	  });
280	  if (res.code === 401) return errorCard_('Not authorized. Check the engine\'s AUXILIO_WORKSPACE_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID.');
281	  if (res.code === 403) return errorCard_('Your domain is not allowed to host visits.');
282	  if (res.code !== 200) return errorCard_('Engine error ' + res.code + '. Try refreshing the page.');
283	
284	  var draft = res.json;
285	  var roster = draft.roster || [];
286	  resolveAndStoreNames_(ev.iCalUid, roster);
287	  var managed = !!draft.materialized;
288	
289	  var card = CardService.newCardBuilder()
290	    .setHeader(CardService.newCardHeader()
291	      .setTitle(managed ? 'Manage passes' : 'Visitor passes')
292	      .setSubtitle(formatEventSubtitle_(ev))
293	      .setImageUrl(ADDON_ICON_URL));
294	
295	  if (roster.length === 0) {
296	    return card
297	      .addSection(CardService.newCardSection()
298	        .addWidget(CardService.newDecoratedText()
299	          .setStartIcon(CardService.newIconImage().setIcon(CardService.Icon.PERSON))
300	          .setText('No guests yet')
301	          .setBottomLabel('Add a guest to this event.')
302	          .setWrapText(true)))
303	      .build();
304	  }
305	
306	  // Stat summary at the top (non-collapsible)
307	  card.addSection(CardService.newCardSection()
308	    .addWidget(CardService.newTextParagraph().setText(buildStatLine_(roster))));
309	
310	  // One collapsible section per guest. The toggle row is always visible;
311	  // name + phone inputs collapse under it to keep long rosters scannable.
312	  var emails = [];
313	  for (var i = 0; i < roster.length; i++) {
314	    var g = roster[i];
315	    emails.push(g.email);
316	
317	    var mainText   = g.name || g.email;
318	    var bottomText = g.name
319	      ? (g.email + '  ·  ' + statusLabel_(g))
320	      : statusLabel_(g);

322	    var sw = CardService.newSwitch()
323	      .setFieldName('inc_' + g.email)
324	      .setValue('on')
325	      .setSelected(g.include !== false);
326	
327	    var guestSection = CardService.newCardSection()
328	      .setCollapsible(true)
329	      .setNumUncollapsibleWidgets(1)

331	      .addWidget(CardService.newDecoratedText()
332	        .setStartIcon(avatarIcon_(g.email, g.name))
333	        .setText(mainText)
334	        .setBottomLabel(bottomText)

336	        .setSwitchControl(sw))

345	        .setText('Phone number')
346	        .setWrapText(true))

349	        .setFieldName('phone_' + g.email)
350	        .setTitle('Phone')

353	    card.addSection(guestSection);
354	  }

356	  var params = { iCalUid: ev.iCalUid, emails: emails.join(',') };
357	  addCalendarSyncSection_(card, draft.calendarSync);
358	
359	  // Primary CTA pinned to the footer — always reachable without scrolling.
360	  card.setFixedFooter(CardService.newFixedFooter()
361	    .setPrimaryButton(CardService.newTextButton()

377	// Background auto-sync (move/delete) status. Only rendered when the host owns the event.
378	function addCalendarSyncSection_(card, sync) {
379	  if (!sync || !sync.relevant) return;
380	  var section = CardService.newCardSection();
381	  if (sync.connected) {
382	    section.addWidget(CardService.newDecoratedText()
383	      .setStartIcon(CardService.newIconImage().setIcon(CardService.Icon.STAR))
384	      .setText('Auto-sync is on')

386	      .setWrapText(true));

389	      .addWidget(CardService.newDecoratedText()
390	        .setStartIcon(CardService.newIconImage().setIcon(CardService.Icon.CLOCK))
391	        .setText('Keep passes in sync')

393	        .setWrapText(true))

397	        .setBackgroundColor(BRAND_COLOR)

405	// ── Actions ───────────────────────────────────────────────
406	// Same derivation the engine uses, so we can tell an auto-derived name from a real one.
407	function cardDeriveName_(email) {
408	  var local = (email.split('@')[0] || email);
409	  return local.replace(/[._-]+/g, ' ').replace(/\b\w/g, function (c) { return c.toUpperCase(); }).trim() || email;
410	}
411	
412	var _peopleWarmed = false;
413	function peopleSearch_(path, email) {
414	  var headers = { Authorization: 'Bearer ' + ScriptApp.getOAuthToken() };
415	  var base = 'https://people.googleapis.com/v1/';
416	  // searchContacts/otherContacts.search require a warmup (empty-query) call first.
417	  if (!_peopleWarmed) {
418	    UrlFetchApp.fetch(base + 'people:searchContacts?query=&readMask=names,emailAddresses', { headers: headers, muteHttpExceptions: true });
419	    UrlFetchApp.fetch(base + 'otherContacts:search?query=&readMask=names,emailAddresses', { headers: headers, muteHttpExceptions: true });
420	    _peopleWarmed = true;
421	    Utilities.sleep(200);
422	  }
423	  var url = base + path + '?query=' + encodeURIComponent(email) + '&readMask=names,emailAddresses';
424	  var resp = UrlFetchApp.fetch(url, { headers: headers, muteHttpExceptions: true });
425	  if (resp.getResponseCode() !== 200) return '';
426	  var results = (JSON.parse(resp.getContentText()).results) || [];
427	  for (var i = 0; i < results.length; i++) {
428	    var person = results[i].person || {};
429	    var emails = person.emailAddresses || [];
430	    var match = emails.some(function (em) { return (em.value || '').toLowerCase() === email.toLowerCase(); });
431	    if (match && person.names && person.names.length) return person.names[0].displayName || '';
432	  }
433	  return '';
434	}
435	
436	function resolveContactName_(email) {
437	  try {
438	    return peopleSearch_('people:searchContacts', email) || peopleSearch_('otherContacts:search', email);
439	  } catch (err) {
440	    return '';
441	  }
442	}
443	
444	// For each guest whose name is still auto-derived, look up the real contact name
445	// and PATCH it onto the draft (so subsequent opens skip the lookup). Mutates roster.
446	function resolveAndStoreNames_(iCalUid, roster) {
447	  var patches = [];
448	  for (var i = 0; i < roster.length; i++) {
449	    var g = roster[i];
450	    var looksDerived = !g.name || g.name === cardDeriveName_(g.email);
451	    if (!looksDerived) continue;
452	    var resolved = resolveContactName_(g.email);
453	    if (resolved && resolved !== g.name) {
454	      g.name = resolved;                       // reflect immediately in this render
455	      patches.push({ email: g.email, name: resolved });
456	    }
457	  }
458	  if (patches.length) {
459	    engineFetch_('/api/visitor/calendar/addon/draft', 'PATCH', { iCalUid: iCalUid, patch: { guests: patches } });
460	  }
461	}
462	
463	// Review step: persist edits, then push a textual confirm card. Cards can't
464	// render HTML inline (that's the "Preview email" overlay link), so the review
465	// here is the recipient list + what will happen — the guard before sending.
466	function onReviewSend(e) {
467	  var iCalUid = e.parameters.iCalUid;
468	  patchFromForm_(iCalUid, e); // persist toggles/names/phones BEFORE leaving the form card
469	  var emails = (e.parameters.emails || '').split(',').filter(Boolean_);
470	  var inputs = formInputs_(e);
471	  var included = [];
472	  var excluded = [];
473	  for (var i = 0; i < emails.length; i++) {
474	    var em = emails[i];
475	    var on = formValue_(inputs['inc_' + em]) === 'on';
476	    var name = formValue_(inputs['name_' + em]) || em;
477	    (on ? included : excluded).push({ email: em, name: name });
478	  }
479	  if (!included.length) return notify_('Turn on at least one guest before sending.');
480	  return CardService.newActionResponseBuilder()
481	    .setNavigation(CardService.newNavigation().pushCard(buildConfirmCard_(e.parameters, included, excluded)))
482	    .build();
483	}
484	
485	function buildConfirmCard_(params, included, excluded) {
486	  var card = CardService.newCardBuilder()
487	    .setHeader(CardService.newCardHeader()
488	      .setTitle('Send ' + included.length + ' pass' + (included.length > 1 ? 'es' : '') + '?')
489	      .setImageUrl(ADDON_ICON_URL));
490	  var sec = CardService.newCardSection().setHeader('These guests get a pass');
491	  for (var i = 0; i < included.length; i++) {
492	    sec.addWidget(CardService.newDecoratedText()
493	      .setStartIcon(CardService.newIconImage().setIcon(CardService.Icon.INVITE))
494	      .setText(included[i].name)
495	      .setBottomLabel(included[i].email)
496	      .setWrapText(true));
497	  }
498	  card.addSection(sec);
499	  if (excluded.length) {
500	    var ex = CardService.newCardSection().setHeader('Skipped (no pass / cancelled if sent before)');
501	    for (var j = 0; j < excluded.length; j++) {
502	      ex.addWidget(CardService.newDecoratedText()
503	        .setText(excluded[j].name).setBottomLabel(excluded[j].email).setWrapText(true));
504	    }
505	    card.addSection(ex);
506	  }
507	  card.setFixedFooter(CardService.newFixedFooter()
508	    .setPrimaryButton(CardService.newTextButton()
509	      .setText('Send passes')
510	      .setTextButtonStyle(CardService.TextButtonStyle.FILLED)
511	      .setBackgroundColor(BRAND_COLOR)
512	      .setOnClickAction(CardService.newAction().setFunctionName('onSaveAndSend').setParameters(params)))
513	    .setSecondaryButton(CardService.newTextButton()
514	      .setText('Back')
515	      .setOnClickAction(CardService.newAction().setFunctionName('onConfirmBack').setParameters(params))));
516	  return card.build();
517	}
518	
519	function onConfirmBack(e) {
520	  return CardService.newActionResponseBuilder()
521	    .setNavigation(CardService.newNavigation().popCard())
522	    .build();
523	}
524	
525	function onSaveAndSend(e) {
526	  var iCalUid = e.parameters.iCalUid;
527	  patchFromForm_(iCalUid, e);                       // persist toggles/name/phone first
528	  var res = engineFetch_('/api/visitor/calendar/addon/send', 'POST', { iCalUid: iCalUid });
529	  var msg;
530	  if (res.code === 200) {
531	    var sent = (res.json.created || []).length;
532	    var cancelled = (res.json.cancelled || []).length;
533	    var failed = res.json.failed || [];
534	    if (failed.length) {
535	      // Surface WHY a pass couldn't be sent (most often: event time in the past)
536	      // instead of silently leaving the guest pending.
537	      var reason = (failed[0] && failed[0].reason) || 'could not be sent';
538	      msg = "Couldn't send " + failed.length + ' pass' + (failed.length > 1 ? 'es' : '') + ': ' + reason;
539	    } else {
540	      var parts = [];
541	      if (sent) parts.push(sent + ' sent');
542	      if (cancelled) parts.push(cancelled + ' cancelled');
543	      msg = parts.length ? ('Passes updated: ' + parts.join(', ') + '.') : 'No changes.';
544	    }
545	  } else {
546	    msg = 'Update failed (' + res.code + ').';
547	  }
548	  // Re-render so the card switches to Manage mode and shows fresh statuses.
549	  return CardService.newActionResponseBuilder()
550	    .setNotification(CardService.newNotification().setText(msg))
551	    .setNavigation(CardService.newNavigation().updateCard(buildVisitorCard_(e)))
552	    .build();
553	}
554	
555	function onPreview(e) {
556	  var iCalUid = e.parameters.iCalUid;
557	  patchFromForm_(iCalUid, e); // capture edits before previewing
558	  var emails = (e.parameters.emails || '').split(',').filter(Boolean_);
559	  var inputs = formInputs_(e);
560	  var target = null;
561	  for (var i = 0; i < emails.length; i++) {
562	    if (formValue_(inputs['inc_' + emails[i]]) === 'on') { target = emails[i]; break; }
563	  }
564	  if (!target) return notify_('Turn on at least one guest to preview their email.');
565	
566	  var pv = engineFetch_('/api/visitor/calendar/addon/preview', 'POST', { iCalUid: iCalUid, visitorEmail: target });
567	  if (pv.code !== 200 || !pv.json.previewToken) return notify_('Preview failed (' + pv.code + ').');
568	
569	  // Open the REAL branded HTML in a popup overlay (the card can't render HTML).
570	  var url = ENGINE_BASE + '/api/visitor/calendar/addon/preview-html?t=' + encodeURIComponent(pv.json.previewToken);
571	  return CardService.newActionResponseBuilder()
572	    .setOpenLink(CardService.newOpenLink()
573	      .setUrl(url)
574	      .setOpenAs(CardService.OpenAs.OVERLAY)
575	      .setOnClose(CardService.OnClose.NOTHING))
576	    .build();
577	}
578	
579	// ── Helpers ───────────────────────────────────────────────
580	function patchFromForm_(iCalUid, e) {
581	  var emails = (e.parameters.emails || '').split(',').filter(Boolean_);
582	  var inputs = formInputs_(e);
583	  var guests = emails.map(function (em) {
584	    return {
585	      email: em,
586	      include: formValue_(inputs['inc_' + em]) === 'on', // OFF switches are absent → false
587	      name: formValue_(inputs['name_' + em]),
588	      phone: formValue_(inputs['phone_' + em])
589	    };
590	  });
591	  return engineFetch_('/api/visitor/calendar/addon/draft', 'PATCH', { iCalUid: iCalUid, patch: { guests: guests } });
592	}
593	
594	function formInputs_(e) {
595	  return (e.commonEventObject && e.commonEventObject.formInputs) || {};
596	}
597	function formValue_(input) {
598	  if (input && input.stringInputs && input.stringInputs.value && input.stringInputs.value.length) {
599	    return input.stringInputs.value[0];
600	  }
601	  return '';
602	}
603	function Boolean_(x) { return !!x; }
604	
605	function textPreview_(json) {
606	  var t = json.text || json.html || '';
607	  t = t.replace(/<[^>]+>/g, ' ').replace(/&nbsp;/g, ' ').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();
608	  return t.length > 1200 ? t.substring(0, 1200) + '…' : t;
609	}
610	
611	function notify_(msg) {
612	  return CardService.newActionResponseBuilder()
613	    .setNotification(CardService.newNotification().setText(msg))
614	    .build();
615	}
616	
617	function errorCard_(msg) {
618	  return CardService.newCardBuilder()
619	    .setHeader(CardService.newCardHeader()
620	      .setTitle('Something went wrong')
621	      .setImageUrl(ADDON_ICON_URL))
622	    .addSection(CardService.newCardSection()
623	      .addWidget(CardService.newDecoratedText()
624	        .setStartIcon(CardService.newIconImage().setIcon(CardService.Icon.DESCRIPTION))
625	        .setText(msg)
626	        .setWrapText(true)))
627	    .build();
628	}

1	/**
2	 * Visit Draft store — the curated visitor-invite roster for the Auxilio Workspace
3	 * calendar add-on.
4	 *
5	 * The add-on builds/loads a draft per event (keyed by iCalUID), the host edits it
6	 * in the card, and on send we materialize invites from the INCLUDED guests via the
7	 * shared `createVisitorInvite` (same call every other channel uses — no logic fork).
8	 *
9	 * Once materialized, `calendar_event_links.draft_authoritative=1` (written by the
10	 * caller, not here) makes this the source of truth: passive producers (.ics / OAuth
11	 * poll) then only retime/cancel — they re-issue from this roster, never raw attendees.
12	 *
13	 * Spec: spec/2026-06-16_calendar_addon_register_endpoint.md §4.
14	 * NOTE: this module must NOT import calendar-ingest (calendar-ingest imports this).
15	 */
16	
17	import { getDb } from '../../lib/database';
18	import { createVisitorInvite, notifyInviteCancelled, RESCHEDULED_EMAIL_OVERRIDE } from './invite-manager';
19	import { cancelInvitationById, findActiveInvitationForVisitor, getActiveInvitationById } from './db';
20	import { isInvitationLinkedToOtherEvent, rekeyCalendarLink, isVisitorAttendee } from './calendar-ingest';
21	import { resolveHostContactByEmail } from './host-resolution';

85	// ─── IST helpers (mirror calendar-ingest; createVisitorInvite expects IST date/time) ──
86	
87	const IST_DATE = new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-CA', {
88	  timeZone: 'Asia/Kolkata',
89	  year: 'numeric',
90	  month: '2-digit',
91	  day: '2-digit',
92	});
93	const IST_TIME = new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-GB', {
94	  timeZone: 'Asia/Kolkata',
95	  hour: '2-digit',
96	  minute: '2-digit',
97	  hour12: false,
98	});
99	const istDate = (d: Date): string => IST_DATE.format(d);
100	const istTime = (d: Date): string => IST_TIME.format(d);
101	
102	function nameFromEmail(email: string): string {
103	  const local = email.split('@')[0] ?? email;
104	  return (
105	    local
106	      .replace(/[._-]+/g, ' ')
107	      .replace(/\b\w/g, (c) => c.toUpperCase())
108	      .trim() || email
109	  );
110	}

112	// ─── Row mapping ──────────────────────────────────────────────
113	
114	interface DraftRow {
115	  ical_uid: string;
116	  tenant_id: string | null;
117	  provider_event_id: string | null;
118	  title: string | null;
119	  host_email: string | null;
120	  location: string | null;
121	  roster: string | null;
122	  event_start: string | null;
123	  event_end: string | null;
124	  materialized: number | null;
125	}
126	
127	function rowToDraft(row: DraftRow): VisitDraft {
128	  let roster: DraftGuest[] = [];
129	  try {
130	    const parsed = JSON.parse(row.roster ?? '[]');
131	    if (Array.isArray(parsed)) roster = parsed as DraftGuest[];
132	  } catch {
133	    /* corrupt JSON → empty roster */
134	  }
135	  return {
136	    iCalUid: row.ical_uid,
137	    tenantId: row.tenant_id ?? DEFAULT_TENANT_ID,
138	    providerEventId: row.provider_event_id ?? undefined,
139	    title: row.title ?? undefined,
140	    hostEmail: row.host_email ?? undefined,
141	    location: row.location ?? undefined,
142	    roster,
143	    eventStart: row.event_start ?? undefined,
144	    eventEnd: row.event_end ?? undefined,
145	    materialized: !!row.materialized,
146	  };
147	}
148	
149	// ─── Persistence ──────────────────────────────────────────────
150	
151	export async function getDraft(iCalUid: string): Promise<VisitDraft | null> {
152	  const db = await getDb();
153	  const result = await db.execute({
154	    sql: `SELECT * FROM visit_drafts WHERE ical_uid = ? LIMIT 1`,
155	    args: [iCalUid],
156	  });
157	  const row = result.rows[0] as unknown as DraftRow | undefined;
158	  return row ? rowToDraft(row) : null;
159	}
160	
161	/**
162	 * Fallback lookup by Google's stable event id. `Events.get` is flaky, so the same
163	 * event can be keyed on the real iCalUID one open and on the eventId fallback the
164	 * next — `provider_event_id` (the Google event.id) is stable across both, so this
165	 * recovers the existing draft and prevents key-drift from rebuilding it fresh
166	 * (which would wipe the host's toggle/edit state).
167	 */
168	export async function getDraftByProviderEventId(providerEventId: string): Promise<VisitDraft | null> {
169	  if (!providerEventId) return null;
170	  const db = await getDb();
171	  const result = await db.execute({
172	    sql: `SELECT * FROM visit_drafts WHERE provider_event_id = ? LIMIT 1`,
173	    args: [providerEventId],
174	  });
175	  const row = result.rows[0] as unknown as DraftRow | undefined;
176	  return row ? rowToDraft(row) : null;
177	}
178	
179	/** Re-key a draft to a newly-resolved iCalUID (Events.get recovered after a fallback). */
180	export async function rekeyDraft(oldICalUid: string, newICalUid: string): Promise<void> {
181	  if (!oldICalUid || !newICalUid || oldICalUid === newICalUid) return;
182	  const db = await getDb();
183	  // Delete any empty placeholder under the new key first, then move the row.
184	  await db.execute({ sql: `DELETE FROM visit_drafts WHERE ical_uid = ?`, args: [newICalUid] });
185	  await db.execute({
186	    sql: `UPDATE visit_drafts SET ical_uid = ?, updated_at = now()::text WHERE ical_uid = ?`,
187	    args: [newICalUid, oldICalUid],
188	  });
189	}
190	
191	export async function upsertDraft(draft: VisitDraft): Promise<void> {
192	  const db = await getDb();
193	  await db.execute({
194	    sql: `INSERT INTO visit_drafts (ical_uid, tenant_id, provider_event_id, title, host_email, location, roster, event_start, event_end, materialized, updated_at)
195	          VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, now()::text)
196	          ON CONFLICT (ical_uid) DO UPDATE SET
197	            tenant_id = EXCLUDED.tenant_id,
198	            provider_event_id = COALESCE(EXCLUDED.provider_event_id, visit_drafts.provider_event_id),
199	            title = EXCLUDED.title,
200	            host_email = EXCLUDED.host_email,
201	            location = EXCLUDED.location,
202	            roster = EXCLUDED.roster,
203	            event_start = EXCLUDED.event_start,
204	            event_end = EXCLUDED.event_end,
205	            materialized = EXCLUDED.materialized,
206	            updated_at = now()::text`,
207	    args: [
208	      draft.iCalUid,
209	      draft.tenantId,
210	      draft.providerEventId ?? null,
211	      draft.title ?? null,
212	      draft.hostEmail ?? null,
213	      draft.location ?? null,
214	      JSON.stringify(draft.roster),
215	      draft.eventStart ?? null,
216	      draft.eventEnd ?? null,
217	      draft.materialized ? 1 : 0,
218	    ],
219	  });
220	}
221	
222	// ─── Build / load ─────────────────────────────────────────────
223	
224	/** Most recent phone we've seen for this email — pre-fill from visitor history. */
225	async function lastKnownPhoneFor(email: string): Promise<string | undefined> {
226	  const db = await getDb();
227	  const result = await db.execute({
228	    sql: `SELECT visitor_phone FROM visitor_invitations
229	          WHERE lower(visitor_email) = ? AND visitor_phone IS NOT NULL AND visitor_phone <> ''
230	          ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT 1`,
231	    args: [email.toLowerCase()],
232	  });
233	  const row = result.rows[0] as unknown as { visitor_phone?: string } | undefined;
234	  return row?.visitor_phone || undefined;
235	}
236	
237	export type PhoneLookup = (email: string) => Promise<string | undefined>;
238	/** Decides each guest's DEFAULT toggle. Defaults to "everyone on" when omitted. */
239	export type IncludeDecider = (email: string) => boolean;
240	
241	/**
242	 * Build the curated roster from raw attendees: drop the host + blanks, dedup
243	 * case-insensitively, derive a name when absent, pre-fill phone via `phoneLookup`,
244	 * and set each guest's DEFAULT toggle via `includeDecider` (so same-workspace /
245	 * same-location colleagues come pre-toggled-OFF — same rule as every other channel).
246	 * Pure (DB + classification injected) → unit-testable.
247	 */
248	export async function buildRoster(
249	  attendees: { email: string; name?: string }[],
250	  hostEmail: string | undefined,
251	  phoneLookup: PhoneLookup,
252	  includeDecider: IncludeDecider = () => true,
253	): Promise<DraftGuest[]> {
254	  const host = hostEmail?.toLowerCase();
255	  const seen = new Set<string>();
256	  const roster: DraftGuest[] = [];
257	  for (const attendee of attendees) {
258	    const email = attendee.email?.toLowerCase();
259	    if (!email || email === host || seen.has(email)) continue;
260	    seen.add(email);
261	    roster.push({
262	      email,
263	      name: attendee.name?.trim() || nameFromEmail(email),
264	      phone: await phoneLookup(email),
265	      include: includeDecider(email),
266	      status: 'pending',
267	    });
268	  }
269	  return roster;
270	}
271	
272	/**
273	 * Adopt passes that already exist for these guests (created via ANY channel —
274	 * dashboard, Slack, Excel, or a prior add-on send) so the add-on never offers a
275	 * DUPLICATE. Mutates each guest in place: sets status='sent' + invitationId when an
276	 * active pass is found. Two matchers, cheapest first:
277	 *   1. Exact: our host-invite .ics uses UID `<invitationId>@appointy-visitor`; if
278	 *      Google preserved that UID, resolve the invitation id directly.
279	 *   2. Identity: an active invitation for the same visitor_email on the event's date.
280	 * Returns true if any guest was adopted (→ caller marks the draft materialized).
281	 */
282	async function adoptExistingPasses(roster: DraftGuest[], iCalUid: string, eventDate: string): Promise<boolean> {
283	  // (1) Exact UID match — only when the event key looks like our own ICS UID.
284	  const uidMatch = /^(.+)@appointy-visitor$/.exec(iCalUid ?? '');
285	  const exactInviteId = uidMatch?.[1];
286	  let adopted = false;
287	
288	  for (const guest of roster) {
289	    if (guest.status === 'sent' && guest.invitationId) continue; // already linked
290	    let existing = exactInviteId ? await getActiveInvitationById(exactInviteId).catch(() => null) : null;
291	    // Exact match must actually be THIS guest's pass (UID identifies the visit, but
292	    // an event could have several guests — fall back to identity if it isn't them).
293	    if (existing && existing.visitor_email?.toLowerCase() !== guest.email.toLowerCase()) existing = null;
294	    if (!existing && eventDate) {
295	      const candidate = await findActiveInvitationForVisitor(guest.email, eventDate).catch(() => null);
296	      // Only adopt by identity if the pass isn't already owned by ANOTHER event —
297	      // otherwise a new event would wrongly inherit a different meeting's pass.
298	      if (candidate && !(await isInvitationLinkedToOtherEvent(candidate.id, iCalUid).catch(() => false))) {
299	        existing = candidate;
300	      }
301	    }
302	    if (existing) {
303	      guest.invitationId = existing.id;
304	      guest.status = 'sent';
305	      // A guest who already has a pass IS included, regardless of the classifier's
306	      // default-off (e.g. a same-location colleague the host deliberately invited).
307	      // Otherwise a later "Update" would cancel their adopted pass.
308	      guest.include = true;
309	      if (!guest.name || guest.name.toLowerCase() === guest.email.toLowerCase()) {
310	        guest.name = existing.visitor_name || guest.name;
311	      }
312	      if (!guest.phone && existing.visitor_phone) guest.phone = existing.visitor_phone;
313	      adopted = true;
314	    }
315	  }
316	  return adopted;
317	}
318	
319	/** IST date (YYYY-MM-DD) for an event start, to match invitations.expected_date. */
320	function eventDateIST(eventStart?: string): string {
321	  return eventStart ? istDate(new Date(eventStart)) : istDate(new Date());
322	}
323	
324	/**
325	 * A pass duration ALWAYS equals the calendar event's start/end (decision
326	 * 2026-06-17). Returns valid dates, or null when the event times are missing —
327	 * callers must then NOT guess (no "now/+1h" fallback), and surface a failure
328	 * instead. Allows date-only (all-day) values.
329	 */
330	function resolveEventWindow(draft: VisitDraft): { start: Date; end: Date } | null {
331	  if (!draft.eventStart || !draft.eventEnd) return null;
332	  const start = new Date(draft.eventStart);
333	  const end = new Date(draft.eventEnd);
334	  if (Number.isNaN(start.getTime()) || Number.isNaN(end.getTime())) return null;
335	  return { start, end };
336	}
337	
338	const EVENT_TIME_MISSING = 'Could not read the event time — reopen the event and try again.';
339	
340	async function buildDraft(input: BuildDraftInput): Promise<VisitDraft> {
341	  // Default each guest's toggle by the SAME rule as every other channel: external
342	  // or different-office → ON; same-workspace same/no-location → OFF (host can still
343	  // toggle on). isVisitorAttendee resolves the host's office internally.
344	  const includeDecider: IncludeDecider = (email) => isVisitorAttendee(email, input.hostEmail);
345	
346	  const roster = await buildRoster(input.attendees, input.hostEmail, lastKnownPhoneFor, includeDecider);
347	  const adopted = await adoptExistingPasses(roster, input.iCalUid, eventDateIST(input.eventStart));
348	  return {
349	    iCalUid: input.iCalUid,
350	    tenantId: input.tenantId ?? DEFAULT_TENANT_ID,
351	    providerEventId: input.providerEventId,
352	    title: input.title,
353	    hostEmail: input.hostEmail?.toLowerCase(),
354	    location: input.location,
355	    roster,
356	    eventStart: input.eventStart,
357	    eventEnd: input.eventEnd,
358	    // If a pass already exists for any guest, open straight into Manage mode.
359	    materialized: adopted,
360	  };
361	}
362	
363	export interface GetOrCreateOpts {
364	  /**
365	   * When true (the trusted card-open path), guests removed from the event are
366	   * dropped from the roster and any sent pass is cancelled. When false (the
367	   * OAuth poll — which can deliver partial attendee lists), removals are NOT
368	   * acted on: existing guests are kept as-is. Decision 2026-06-17 (plan §8B).
369	   */
370	  reconcileRemovals?: boolean;
371	}
372	
373	/**
374	 * Load-or-build the draft for an event, keeping it in sync with the current
375	 * attendee list.
376	 * - No draft → build + persist from the incoming attendees.
377	 * - Existing draft → add newly-invited attendees (as pending/included), preserve
378	 *   the host's edits (include toggle, name, phone, status, invitationId) for
379	 *   guests already present, and reconcile removals per `reconcileRemovals`.
380	 */
381	export async function getOrCreateDraft(
382	  input: BuildDraftInput,
383	  opts: GetOrCreateOpts = {},
384	): Promise<VisitDraft> {
385	  const built = await buildDraft(input);
386	  let existing = await getDraft(input.iCalUid);
387	
388	  // Key-drift recovery: if not found by iCalUid but we have the stable Google
389	  // event id, find the draft under its old key and re-key it to the now-known
390	  // iCalUid. Prevents a flaky Events.get from rebuilding a fresh draft (which would
391	  // wipe the host's toggle/edit state).
392	  if (!existing && input.providerEventId) {
393	    const byProvider = await getDraftByProviderEventId(input.providerEventId);
394	    if (byProvider && byProvider.iCalUid !== input.iCalUid) {
395	      // Move BOTH the draft and its ledger row onto the now-resolved iCalUid so
396	      // every later lookup (send, poll reschedule, cancel) stays consistent.
397	      await rekeyDraft(byProvider.iCalUid, input.iCalUid);
398	      await rekeyCalendarLink(byProvider.iCalUid, input.iCalUid).catch((e) => console.warn('[VisitDraft] link rekey failed:', e));
399	      existing = await getDraft(input.iCalUid);
400	    } else if (byProvider) {
401	      existing = byProvider;
402	    }
403	  }
404	
405	  if (!existing) {
406	    await upsertDraft(built);
407	    return built;
408	  }
409	
410	  const prevByEmail: Record<string, DraftGuest> = {};
411	  for (const g of existing.roster) prevByEmail[g.email.toLowerCase()] = g;
412	  const incoming = new Set(built.roster.map((g) => g.email.toLowerCase()));
413	
414	  // Safety: an EMPTY incoming list means "unknown" (a transient/partial payload),
415	  // not "every guest was removed" — never mass-cancel on it. Only reconcile
416	  // removals when we actually received attendees.
417	  const reconcileRemovals = !!opts.reconcileRemovals && built.roster.length > 0;
418	
419	  // 1) Current attendees first (new ones added; existing ones keep their edits).
420	  const roster: DraftGuest[] = built.roster.map((g) => {
421	    const prev = prevByEmail[g.email.toLowerCase()];
422	    if (!prev) return g;
423	    const name = prev.name && prev.name.toLowerCase() !== prev.email.toLowerCase() ? prev.name : g.name;
424	    return { ...prev, name };
425	  });
426	
427	  // 2) Guests in the draft but no longer on the event.
428	  for (const prev of existing.roster) {
429	    if (incoming.has(prev.email.toLowerCase())) continue;
430	    if (reconcileRemovals) {
431	      // Trusted card path: cancel a sent pass, then drop the guest from the list.
432	      if (prev.status === 'sent' && prev.invitationId) {
433	        console.log(`[VisitDraft] guest ${prev.email} removed from event → cancelling pass ${prev.invitationId}`);
434	        const ok = await cancelInvitationById(prev.invitationId).catch((e) => {
435	          console.warn(`[VisitDraft] cancel-on-remove ${prev.invitationId} failed:`, e);
436	          return false;
437	        });
438	        if (ok) await notifyInviteCancelled(prev.invitationId, { reason: 'You were removed from the calendar event.' });
439	      } else {
440	        console.log(`[VisitDraft] guest ${prev.email} removed from event (no active pass to cancel)`);
441	      }
442	      // dropped (not pushed)
443	    } else {
444	      // Conservative poll path: keep the guest untouched.
445	      roster.push(prev);
446	    }
447	  }
448	
449	  const refreshed: VisitDraft = {
450	    ...existing,
451	    title: input.title ?? existing.title,
452	    location: existing.location ?? input.location,
453	    eventStart: input.eventStart ?? existing.eventStart,
454	    eventEnd: input.eventEnd ?? existing.eventEnd,
455	    providerEventId: input.providerEventId ?? existing.providerEventId,
456	    roster,
457	    materialized: existing.materialized || roster.some((g) => g.status === 'sent'),
458	  };
459	  await upsertDraft(refreshed);
460	  return refreshed;
461	}
462	
463	/**
464	 * Resolve a host's display name from the Workspace-linked staff registry by email.
465	 * The add-on's ID token carries no usable name claim (no profile scope), so we
466	 * never trust the email-derived fallback when a real staff name exists.
467	 */
468	export async function resolveHostDisplayName(email: string | undefined, fallback?: string): Promise<string | undefined> {
469	  if (!email) return fallback;
470	  const resolved = await resolveHostContactByEmail(email).catch(() => null);
471	  if (resolved?.name && resolved.name.toLowerCase() !== email.toLowerCase()) return resolved.name;
472	  if (fallback && fallback.toLowerCase() !== email.toLowerCase()) return fallback;
473	  return resolved?.name ?? fallback ?? nameFromEmail(email);
474	}
475	
476	/**
477	 * Reflect an externally-cancelled invitation (e.g. dashboard cancel button) in any
478	 * draft that holds it: mark the guest cancelled + exclude them, so a later
479	 * reschedule/Update doesn't recreate their pass (F1).
480	 */
481	export async function markDraftGuestCancelledByInvitationId(invitationId: string): Promise<void> {
482	  if (!invitationId) return;
483	  const db = await getDb();
484	  const result = await db.execute({
485	    sql: `SELECT ical_uid FROM visit_drafts WHERE roster LIKE ?`,
486	    args: [`%"${invitationId}"%`],
487	  });
488	  for (const row of result.rows as unknown as { ical_uid: string }[]) {
489	    const draft = await getDraft(row.ical_uid);
490	    if (!draft) continue;
491	    let changed = false;
492	    for (const g of draft.roster) {
493	      if (g.invitationId === invitationId) {
494	        g.status = 'cancelled';
495	        g.include = false;
496	        g.invitationId = undefined;
497	        changed = true;
498	      }
499	    }
500	    if (changed) await upsertDraft(draft);
501	  }
502	}
503	
504	/**
505	 * Refresh only the event times on an existing draft (no-op if absent). Used by the
506	 * passive ingest on reschedule/redelivery — keeps times current WITHOUT touching the
507	 * host's curated roster.
508	 */
509	export async function touchDraftTimes(
510	  iCalUid: string,
511	  eventStart?: string,
512	  eventEnd?: string,
513	): Promise<void> {
514	  const db = await getDb();
515	  await db.execute({
516	    sql: `UPDATE visit_drafts SET event_start = ?, event_end = ?, updated_at = now()::text WHERE ical_uid = ?`,
517	    args: [eventStart ?? null, eventEnd ?? null, iCalUid],
518	  });
519	}
520	
521	/** Apply a host edit (per-guest fields / include toggle / visit location) and persist. */
522	export async function patchDraft(iCalUid: string, patch: DraftPatch | undefined): Promise<VisitDraft | null> {
523	  const draft = await getDraft(iCalUid);
524	  if (!draft) return null;
525	  if (patch?.location !== undefined) draft.location = patch.location;
526	  for (const gp of patch?.guests ?? []) {
527	    const guest = draft.roster.find((g) => g.email.toLowerCase() === gp.email.toLowerCase());
528	    if (!guest) continue;
529	    if (gp.include !== undefined) guest.include = gp.include;
530	    if (gp.name !== undefined) guest.name = gp.name;
531	    if (gp.phone !== undefined) guest.phone = gp.phone;
532	    if (gp.passTemplateKey !== undefined) guest.passTemplateKey = gp.passTemplateKey;
533	    if (gp.emailTemplateKey !== undefined) guest.emailTemplateKey = gp.emailTemplateKey;
534	  }
535	  await upsertDraft(draft);
536	  return draft;
537	}
538	
539	/** Record created invites back onto the draft (status + invitationId) and optionally new times. */
540	export async function markMaterialized(
541	  iCalUid: string,
542	  passes: { visitorEmail: string; invitationId: string }[],
543	  times?: { eventStart?: string; eventEnd?: string },
544	): Promise<void> {
545	  const draft = await getDraft(iCalUid);
546	  if (!draft) return;
547	  for (const pass of passes) {
548	    const guest = draft.roster.find((g) => g.email.toLowerCase() === pass.visitorEmail.toLowerCase());
549	    if (guest) {
550	      guest.invitationId = pass.invitationId;
551	      guest.status = 'sent';
552	    }
553	  }
554	  draft.materialized = true;
555	  if (times?.eventStart !== undefined) draft.eventStart = times.eventStart;
556	  if (times?.eventEnd !== undefined) draft.eventEnd = times.eventEnd;
557	  await upsertDraft(draft);
558	}
559	
560	// ─── Materialize ──────────────────────────────────────────────
561	
562	/**
563	 * Create one visitor invite per INCLUDED guest, honoring the host's overrides.
564	 * Reuses `createVisitorInvite` (the shared cross-channel entry) — no logic fork.
565	 * Caller is responsible for cancelling any prior invites and writing the
566	 * `calendar_event_links` ledger row (so dedup/reschedule converge).
567	 */
568	export async function materializeDraft(
569	  draft: VisitDraft,
570	  opts: { hostName?: string; rescheduled?: boolean } = {},
571	): Promise<{ invitationIds: string[]; passes: PassResult[] }> {
572	  // Pass duration must equal the event window — never guess (decision 2026-06-17).
573	  const window = resolveEventWindow(draft);
574	  if (!window) {
575	    console.warn(`[VisitDraft] materialize skipped for ${draft.iCalUid}: ${EVENT_TIME_MISSING}`);
576	    return { invitationIds: [], passes: [] };
577	  }
578	  const { start, end } = window;
579	
580	  const resolvedHost = draft.hostEmail
581	    ? await resolveHostContactByEmail(draft.hostEmail).catch(() => null)
582	    : null;
583	  // Prefer the Workspace-resolved name; only fall back to opts.hostName when it's
584	  // a real name (not the email the ID token gives us). Never show the email.
585	  const hostName = await resolveHostDisplayName(draft.hostEmail, opts.hostName);
586	  // On a reschedule, the guest already had a pass → frame the new one as updated.
587	  const emailOverride = opts.rescheduled ? { ...RESCHEDULED_EMAIL_OVERRIDE } : undefined;
588	
589	  const passes: PassResult[] = [];
590	  for (const guest of draft.roster) {
591	    if (!guest.include || !guest.email) continue;
592	    const visitorName = guest.name?.trim() || nameFromEmail(guest.email);
593	    try {
594	      const result = await createVisitorInvite({
595	        visitorName,
596	        visitorEmail: guest.email,
597	        visitorPhone: guest.phone || undefined,
598	        purpose: draft.title,
599	        hostEmail: draft.hostEmail,
600	        hostEmployeeId: resolvedHost?.staff?.id,
601	        hostEmployeeName: hostName ?? draft.hostEmail,
602	        expectedDate: istDate(start),
603	        expectedTime: istTime(start),
604	        validFrom: start.toISOString(),
605	        validUntil: end.toISOString(),
606	        emailTemplateKey: guest.emailTemplateKey,
607	        emailTemplateOverride: emailOverride,
608	        passTemplateKey: guest.passTemplateKey,
609	        invitedVia: 'calendar',
610	        invitedBy: draft.hostEmail,
611	        approvalTier: 'invited',
612	        // The host explicitly included this guest in the add-on roster → that IS
613	        // the override for the same-workspace guard (F2).
614	        allowInternalColleague: true,
615	      });
616	      passes.push({
617	        visitorEmail: guest.email,
618	        visitorName,
619	        invitationId: result.invitationId,
620	        passUrl: result.qrUrl,
621	        status: 'sent',
622	      });
623	    } catch (err) {
624	      // One bad guest (e.g. past date) shouldn't sink the rest.
625	      console.warn(`[VisitDraft] invite for ${guest.email} failed:`, err);
626	    }
627	  }
628	  return { invitationIds: passes.map((p) => p.invitationId), passes };
629	}
630	
631	export interface ApplyResult {
632	  /** Every currently-active invitation id (for the ledger). */
633	  invitationIds: string[];
634	  /** Guests whose pass was created in THIS apply. */
635	  created: PassResult[];
636	  /** Guests whose pass was cancelled in THIS apply. */
637	  cancelled: { visitorEmail: string; invitationId: string }[];
638	  /** Guests we tried to send but couldn't (e.g. event time in the past). */
639	  failed: { visitorEmail: string; reason: string }[];
640	  draft: VisitDraft;
641	}
642	
643	/**
644	 * Reconcile the live passes to match the roster's include toggles (plan §1):
645	 *   include=true  & no active pass → create
646	 *   include=false & active pass    → cancel
647	 *   include=true  & active pass     → keep (a same-time edit; event-move reissue is
648	 *                                     handled separately in calendar-ingest)
649	 * Persists the draft (status + invitationId per guest, materialized flag). The
650	 * caller writes the `calendar_event_links` ledger from `invitationIds`.
651	 */
652	export async function applyDraft(draft: VisitDraft, opts: { hostName?: string } = {}): Promise<ApplyResult> {
653	  // Pass duration must equal the event window — never guess (decision 2026-06-17).
654	  // When the times are unavailable we still process cancellations, but refuse to
655	  // CREATE a pass with a wrong duration; those guests are reported as failed.
656	  const window = resolveEventWindow(draft);
657	  const start = window?.start;
658	  const end = window?.end;
659	  const resolvedHost = draft.hostEmail
660	    ? await resolveHostContactByEmail(draft.hostEmail).catch(() => null)
661	    : null;
662	  const hostName = await resolveHostDisplayName(draft.hostEmail, opts.hostName);
663	
664	  const created: PassResult[] = [];
665	  const cancelled: { visitorEmail: string; invitationId: string }[] = [];
666	  const failed: { visitorEmail: string; reason: string }[] = [];
667	
668	  for (const guest of draft.roster) {
669	    const hasActivePass = guest.status === 'sent' && !!guest.invitationId;
670	
671	    if (guest.include && !hasActivePass) {
672	      if (!start || !end) {
673	        // No reliable event window → don't issue a wrong-duration pass.
674	        failed.push({ visitorEmail: guest.email, reason: EVENT_TIME_MISSING });
675	        continue;
676	      }
677	      const visitorName = guest.name?.trim() || nameFromEmail(guest.email);
678	      try {
679	        const result = await createVisitorInvite({
680	          visitorName,
681	          visitorEmail: guest.email,
682	          visitorPhone: guest.phone || undefined,
683	          purpose: draft.title,
684	          hostEmail: draft.hostEmail,
685	          hostEmployeeId: resolvedHost?.staff?.id,
686	          hostEmployeeName: hostName ?? draft.hostEmail,
687	          expectedDate: istDate(start),
688	          expectedTime: istTime(start),
689	          validFrom: start.toISOString(),
690	          validUntil: end.toISOString(),
691	          emailTemplateKey: guest.emailTemplateKey,
692	          passTemplateKey: guest.passTemplateKey,
693	          invitedVia: 'calendar',
694	          invitedBy: draft.hostEmail,
695	          approvalTier: 'invited',
696	          allowInternalColleague: true, // host-included guest = override (F2)
697	        });
698	        guest.invitationId = result.invitationId;
699	        guest.status = 'sent';
700	        created.push({ visitorEmail: guest.email, visitorName, invitationId: result.invitationId, passUrl: result.qrUrl, status: 'sent' });
701	      } catch (err) {
702	        const reason = err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err);
703	        console.warn(`[VisitDraft] apply-create for ${guest.email} failed:`, reason);
704	        failed.push({ visitorEmail: guest.email, reason });
705	      }
706	    } else if (!guest.include && hasActivePass) {
707	      const id = guest.invitationId as string;
708	      const ok = await cancelInvitationById(id).catch((e) => {
709	        console.warn(`[VisitDraft] apply-cancel ${id} failed:`, e);
710	        return false;
711	      });
712	      if (ok) await notifyInviteCancelled(id, { cancelledBy: hostName, reason: 'The host cancelled your visitor pass.' });
713	      cancelled.push({ visitorEmail: guest.email, invitationId: id });
714	      guest.status = 'cancelled';
715	      guest.invitationId = undefined;
716	    }
717	  }
718	
719	  const invitationIds = draft.roster
720	    .filter((g) => g.status === 'sent' && g.invitationId)
721	    .map((g) => g.invitationId as string);
722	  draft.materialized = invitationIds.length > 0 || draft.materialized;
723	  await upsertDraft(draft);
724	  return { invitationIds, created, cancelled, failed, draft };
725	}
726

310	
311	// An invitation is "active" (could still be honoured at reception) when it isn't
312	// cancelled/rejected/completed — same set the cancel guard uses.
313	const ACTIVE_STATUS_SQL = `approval_status NOT IN ('cancelled', 'rejected', 'completed')`;
314	
315	/**
316	 * Find an ACTIVE invitation for a visitor on a given date — used by the add-on to
317	 * adopt a pass already created through ANY channel (dashboard/Slack/Excel/prior
318	 * add-on) instead of offering a duplicate. Matches on visitor_email + expected_date
319	 * (IST date string). Most-recent first.
320	 */
321	export async function findActiveInvitationForVisitor(
322	  visitorEmail: string,
323	  expectedDate: string,
324	): Promise<VisitorInvitation | null> {
325	  if (!visitorEmail) return null;
326	  const db = await getDb();
327	  const result = await db.execute({
328	    sql: `SELECT * FROM visitor_invitations
329	          WHERE lower(visitor_email) = ? AND expected_date = ? AND ${ACTIVE_STATUS_SQL}
330	          ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT 1`,
331	    args: [visitorEmail.toLowerCase(), expectedDate],
332	  });
333	  return (result.rows[0] as unknown as VisitorInvitation) ?? null;
334	}
335	
336	/**
337	 * Look up an ACTIVE invitation by its id when it's still honourable. Used for the
338	 * exact-match path: our host-invite `.ics` uses UID `<invitationId>@appointy-visitor`;
339	 * if Google preserves that UID on accept, the add-on can resolve the pass exactly.
340	 */
341	export async function getActiveInvitationById(id: string): Promise<VisitorInvitation | null> {
342	  if (!id) return null;
343	  const db = await getDb();
344	  const result = await db.execute({
345	    sql: `SELECT * FROM visitor_invitations WHERE id = ? AND ${ACTIVE_STATUS_SQL} LIMIT 1`,
346	    args: [id],
347	  });
348	  return (result.rows[0] as unknown as VisitorInvitation) ?? null;
349	}
350	
351	/**
352	 * From a list of invitation ids, return only those still ACTIVE (not
353	 * cancelled/rejected/completed). Used on calendar reschedule so a deliberately
354	 * cancelled pass is NOT resurrected at the new event time. Preserves input order.

265	    providerEventId: input.providerEventId ?? null,

293	function parseInvitationIds(raw: string | null): string[] {
294	  if (!raw) return [];
295	  try {
296	    const arr = JSON.parse(raw);
297	    return Array.isArray(arr) ? arr.filter((x): x is string => typeof x === 'string') : [];
298	  } catch {
299	    return [];
300	  }
301	}

303	// ─── Attendee classification ──────────────────────────────────
304	
305	interface Classified {
306	  host?: CalendarAttendee;
307	  visitors: CalendarAttendee[];
308	}
309	
310	/**
311	 * The employee's home office key — the visitor_locations id they're assigned to
312	 * in the dashboard. Returns undefined when no office is set (treated as "same
313	 * office", i.e. we never place them elsewhere). This is admin-owned data, not
314	 * derived from the Workspace directory, so it reliably distinguishes even
315	 * same-city offices.
316	 */
317	function locationKey(staff?: { homeLocationId?: string }): string | undefined {
318	  const id = staff?.homeLocationId?.trim();
319	  return id ? id : undefined;
320	}
321	
322	/**
323	 * Per-attendee: does this person need a visitor pass (relative to the host)?
324	 *
325	 * - External attendees (non-internal domain) → always a visitor.
326	 * - Internal colleagues (same domain) → a visitor ONLY when based at a DIFFERENT
327	 *   home office than the host. Same office, or unknown office for either side,
328	 *   → NOT a visitor (conservative: never spam passes for local colleagues).
329	 * - The host and our own inbox address are never visitors.

330	 *
331	 * Reused by the passive `classifyAttendees` (poll/.ics) AND the add-on roster
332	 * builder (sets each guest's DEFAULT toggle). `home_location_id` is admin-set.
333	 */
334	export function isVisitorAttendee(
335	  attendeeEmail: string | undefined,
336	  hostEmail: string | undefined,
337	  opts: { domains?: string[]; hostLocation?: string } = {},
338	): boolean {
339	  const email = attendeeEmail?.toLowerCase();
340	  if (!email) return false;
341	  const host = hostEmail?.toLowerCase();
342	  if (email === host) return false;
343	  if (inboxAddresses().includes(email)) return false;
344	
345	  const domains = opts.domains ?? internalDomains();
346	  const isExternal = !domains.includes(domainOf(email));
347	  if (isExternal) return true;
348	
349	  // Internal colleague → visitor only if positively placed at a different office.
350	  const hostLocation = opts.hostLocation
351	    ?? locationKey(host ? getStaffByContact({ email: host }) : undefined);
352	  const colleagueLocation = locationKey(getStaffByContact({ email }));
353	  if (!hostLocation || !colleagueLocation) return false; // unknown → assume local
354	  return colleagueLocation !== hostLocation;
355	}
356	
357	/**
358	 * The same-workspace guard (F2): true when this visitor is an internal colleague
359	 * who should NOT normally get a pass — i.e. an internal-domain email at the SAME or
360	 * UNKNOWN home office as the host. External guests and different-office colleagues
361	 * return false (they legitimately get a pass). Empty/absent email → false (can't
362	 * identify → never block). Inverse of `isVisitorAttendee` restricted to internal.
363	 */
364	export function isSameWorkspaceLocalColleague(
365	  visitorEmail: string | undefined,
366	  hostEmail: string | undefined,
367	): boolean {
368	  const email = visitorEmail?.toLowerCase();
369	  if (!email) return false;
370	  if (!internalDomains().includes(domainOf(email))) return false; // external → allowed
371	  return !isVisitorAttendee(email, hostEmail); // internal + same/unknown office → block
372	}
373	
374	function classifyAttendees(event: NormalizedCalendarEvent, domains: string[]): Classified {
375	  const organizerEmail = event.organizer?.email.toLowerCase();
376	  const hostLocation = locationKey(organizerEmail ? getStaffByContact({ email: organizerEmail }) : undefined);
377	  const visitors = event.attendees.filter((a) =>
378	    isVisitorAttendee(a.email, organizerEmail, { domains, hostLocation }),
379	  );
380	  return { host: event.organizer, visitors };
381	}

385	const IST_DATE = new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-CA', {
386	  timeZone: 'Asia/Kolkata',
387	  year: 'numeric',
388	  month: '2-digit',
389	  day: '2-digit',
390	});
391	const IST_TIME = new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-GB', {
392	  timeZone: 'Asia/Kolkata',
393	  hour: '2-digit',
394	  minute: '2-digit',
395	  hour12: false,
396	});
397	
398	function istDate(d: Date): string {
399	  return IST_DATE.format(d); // YYYY-MM-DD
400	}
401	function istTime(d: Date): string {
402	  return IST_TIME.format(d); // HH:mm
403	}
404	
405	function nameFromEmail(email: string): string {
406	  const local = email.split('@')[0] ?? email;
407	  return local
408	    .replace(/[._-]+/g, ' ')
409	    .replace(/\b\w/g, (c) => c.toUpperCase())
410	    .trim() || email;
411	}

653	      const organizerEmail = body.organizerEmail ? String(body.organizerEmail).toLowerCase() : '';
654	      const isOrganizer = !!organizerEmail && organizerEmail === identity.email.toLowerCase();

7	 * dev/prod DB (gating on DATABASE_URL alone is not enough — it's usually set):

Requires VISITOR_CALENDAR_INTERNAL_DOMAINS=appointy.com.\n  test('an internal colleague is not adopted by the loose identity match', async () => {\n    const { createVisitorInvite } = await import('./invite-manager');\n    const { getOrCreateDraft } = await import('./visit-drafts');\n    const { getCurrentISTDate } = await import('../../lib/time');\n\n    const date = getCurrentISTDate();\n    // Same domain as the host → internal; no home office set → same/unknown office →\n    // default-OFF (not a visitor by default).\n    const colleagueEmail = `colleague-${Date.now()}@appointy.com`;\n    // An unrelated active pass for this colleague on the same date (e.g. a different\n    // visit), NOT linked to this event.\n    await createVisitorInvite({\n      visitorName: 'Colleague',\n      visitorEmail: colleagueEmail,\n      expectedDate: date,\n      invitedVia: 'web',\n      approvalTier: 'invited',\n      approvalStatus: 'approved',\n      allowInternalColleague: true,\n    });\n\n    const draft = await getOrCreateDraft({\n      iCalUid: `internal-${Date.now()}@example.com`,\n      hostEmail: 'host@appointy.com',\n      attendees: [{ email: colleagueEmail, name: 'Colleague' }],\n      eventStart: new Date(`${date}T10:00:00+05:30`).toISOString(),\n      eventEnd: new Date(`${date}T11:00:00+05:30`).toISOString(),\n    });\n\n    const guest = draft.roster.find((g) => g.email === colleagueEmail);\n    expect(guest?.internal).toBe(true);          // badged internal\n    expect(guest?.include).toBe(false);          // toggle stays OFF\n    expect(guest?.status).toBe('pending');       // NOT adopted\n    expect(guest?.invitationId).toBeUndefined();\n    expect(draft.materialized).toBe(false);      // plain event, no \"Pass sent\"\n  });\n\n  // Gap A: an adopted pass on an organizer-owned event gets a calendar_event_links"
}
```

1	---
2	name: project_calendar_visitor_addon
3	description: "Calendar visitor Workspace Add-on — internal-first decision, thin-surface architecture, narrow scopes."
